# modules — decision log

> Why modules is built the way it is. Each entry says what was decided, why, what was rejected and where it is described.
>
> **Rule:** do not re-propose what was rejected without new facts. If a decision has to change, ask the author and update this file. An entry is never rewritten: an amendment with its date at the end of the entry corrects it, or a new entry replaces it, and the old one stays where it is, marked "Superseded".
>
> The repository was created on 2026-10-19 from the template of `spec` v0.1.0 (R21 spec), moved on 2026-10-20 to `spec` v0.2.9 (R01), on 2026-11-02 to v0.6.3 (R02) and v0.6.5 (R06), on 2026-11-06 to v0.9.3 (R09), on 2026-11-07 to v0.9.5 (R18), on 2026-11-08 to v0.9.8 (R19), v0.9.9 (R23), v0.9.10 (R24), v0.9.16 (R27) and v0.12.0 (R28), on 2026-11-11 to v0.15.0 (R30), on 2026-11-12 to v0.16.3 (R31), and on 2026-11-14 to v0.17.2 (R32). How it comes to differ from the template over time — by the tools in the devcontainer, by the rules in RUN.md — is recorded here as a decision, as R46 canvas-api recorded how it differs from its pattern: the CLI of `module-kit` in the `toolchain` stage — R10, the generator of synthetic samples of `canvas` there too — R29. Next number — R34.
>
> Here entries are cited as `R03`, in other repositories as `R03 modules`. The decisions of `spec` are cited as "R18 spec", MathTrail's decisions as "R37 canvas-api", and in a list as "canvas-api: R37, R38". Each log has its own numbers.
>
> The open questions that concern several repositories are in the registers of `spec`: О- in product 13 spec, П- in privacy 9 spec, Т- in repositories 8 spec. What is still to be decided in the component itself is in [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 2.

## The tag of `spec`

**R01. `modules` stands on `spec` v0.2.9: the submodule `spec/` is at this tag, the links to `spec` that are pinned to a tag name it, and what came from the template is as in the template v0.2.9. Since then the documents are checked by `just -f spec/justfile docs-check-repo`.**
- **What the tag decided for `modules`** (`git -C spec diff v0.1.0 v0.2.9`, read 2026-10-20; Д6 in table 4 of the RUN.md of `spec`):
  - R24 spec with its 2026-10-20 amendment — the eval sets of the modules in CI of `modules` are run with a separate key of the platform at the AI provider — only for eval, not with the key of the service account of `api` and not with the author's own key. The platform pays. A job with this key, like any job with a secret, does its work only on a push to `main` and on a pull request from a branch of the repository itself. On a pull request from a fork it is not skipped but fails and names the reason, and the merge waits until a maintainer has read the change and moved it to a branch of the repository; `pull_request_target` is used nowhere. A pull request of Dependabot also comes from a branch of the repository, but it gets no secrets of Actions. Where the key lies and how eval runs on a pull request of Dependabot is Т-28. Its recommendation: the Secret Manager of `infra` through Workload Identity Federation, eval on a pull request of Dependabot — after the change is moved to a branch, and while there is no code of `infra` (П-11), eval runs from the devcontainer, with the key in an environment variable, not in CI. Card 3.10 spec names this key too;
  - R25 spec — the samples page for the first measurement is without hosting: its build is downloaded from a release of `canvas` and opened on the device, and the tasks and media files are put into it at the build, from a release of `modules` through the CLI of `module-kit`. While it works, the page does not go to the network. CI of `canvas` takes the CLI of `module-kit` and a release of `modules` at an exact tag — this is the link "CI tool" (repositories 1, 3 spec), and the releases of `modules` got one more consumer. Children's samples are made with the same page, but without a pull request: they do not get into the repository. The argument against hosting proceeds from the volunteer opening the pull request themselves, on GitHub. Whether the CLI needs a command of its own for open views is decided by the detailing of `module-kit`;
  - R27 spec — the generator of randomness and the derivation of seeds come from `spec`, the same for `module-kit`, the widgets, `engine` and `sim`. The numbers of a templated task are substituted from a seed (concepts 9.3 spec), so for a module's tasks too they are derived by the generator of `spec`, and the algorithm is chosen by epic E2 in the RUN.md of `spec` (Т-18). This is an input of T03: a task that comes over from standalone as a template gets its numbers from this generator;
  - the opening of architecture 3 spec — the card is edited for an item of the detailing beyond it by the repository's own "outcome of the detailing", as a row «в реестр spec» of its table 3, once the decision is made, not in advance. For `modules` such an item is the port of standalone's tasks ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 2.3);
  - the answer to В8 `spec` — the "package manifest" in card 3.10 spec is not the module manifest `manifest` of concepts 15 spec. This part of the package will get a term of its own, without the word "manifest", with the package's schema (Т-15), in epic E2 in the RUN.md of `spec`.
- **What the tag brings to every repository:**
  - R30 spec with its amendments — the CI checks are the same for all repositories: each has its own and the shared ones from `.github` — the secret scan, DCO, CodeQL, Scorecard and the documents check. All of them but Scorecard hold the merge into `main`, the quality gate of SonarCloud and the rule for the findings of CodeQL too. Merges are made only by a merge commit: the DCO check looks at each commit of a pull request except merge commits, and exactly those land on `main`. The shared checks will be connected by T21 `spec`: the CI files — calls of the shared workflows, `dependabot.yml`, `.sonarcloud.properties` with the exclusion `spec/**` — are written here by its session, and it also sets the ruleset of the branch `main` (Д7 in table 4 of the RUN.md of `spec`). Until then only the automatic analysis of SonarCloud works for `modules`;
  - R33 spec and rule 12 of RUN.md — the findings of SonarCloud, CodeQL and Scorecard on the previous task are closed before a new one. A finding that a change cannot close stays open, and its reason is recorded: one common to all repositories — in R33 spec, an own one — in this log;
  - R32 spec — the documents are checked by `docs-check` of the `spec` that the submodule stands on: the links, the own sections and the log, the tables «В» and «Д», and the citations of `spec` — by the submodule at its tag. An example of a citation is written in inline code so that the check does not take it for a citation (RULES.md, Citations);
  - R31 spec — a release of `spec` comes out by itself, after a green CI on `main`: the next patch of the line, immutable, with a GitHub attestation. A repository still moves to a new tag in a task of its own;
  - RULES.md, Hard rules — a development or CI tool that ships nowhere and puts none of its own code into what ships may also carry another OSI-approved license or free terms for open source, but only as a named exception: in the license check or in the decision that brings it in (R12 spec);
  - devcontainer — the base image `devcontainers/base` 3.0.10 by digest; Claude Code is installed without the packages' scripts, its `install.cjs` is run by name, and `claude --version` stops the build on a stub; `git.alwaysSignOff` — a commit from Source Control is signed off by DCO (R22 spec, R30 spec);
  - R23, R26, R28 and R29 spec — about other components: the corridor of success, the account with the owner profile and the children's profiles, the graph's types, the reason codes. They change no documents of `modules`.
- **What changed here:**
  - `spec/` is at v0.2.9, commit 5eafb1a. The links to `spec` in README.md and the tag in CLAUDE.md, at the start of RUN.md and in the status of [architecture.md](architecture.md) are v0.2.9. That the repository was created from the template v0.1.0 the opening of the log still says: this is history;
  - the template: the examples of citations of the old template are in inline code, in CLAUDE.md, Documents, in «Ссылках» of RUN.md and at the opening of the log. Before the change `docs-check-repo` gave five errors on them — one per line with such examples. Rule 6 of RUN.md and the section Environment in CLAUDE.md name `docs-check-repo`, and rule 12 of RUN.md is new. These places are as in the template v0.2.9;
  - `.devcontainer/` is the one that the recipe of `spec` v0.2.9 (`editDevcontainer`) builds for `modules` of the kind `docs`. The name of the container and of the network and `postStartCommand` are its own, as at v0.1.0. The move brings in no differences from the template;
  - what the tag decided for `modules` is named in its documents: T01 — R25 spec; T02 — R24 spec with its amendment, Т-28, R25 spec and R30 spec; T03 — R27 spec; T04 — the edit of the card for section 2.3; «Не входит» of epic E1 — the shared CI checks; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 1, 2, 2.1, 2.2 and 2.3. The component's role in CLAUDE.md is not touched: it is a summary, and the details are in [architecture.md](architecture.md).

— *Why now:* T21 `spec` connects the shared checks from `.github` here (R30 spec), and among them the required "Documents / docs-check-repo". The shared `documents.yml` builds the `toolchain` stage from the `.devcontainer/Dockerfile` of whoever called it and runs `just -f spec/justfile docs-check-repo` in it ([`documents.yml`](https://github.com/altnovatrail/.github/blob/ff2a359/.github/workflows/documents.yml), release v0.1.0 of `.github`, commit ff2a359, read 2026-10-20). And this recipe is in `spec` only since v0.2.3 (R32 spec): at v0.1.0 the required check would be red and would hold any merge. v0.2.9 is the latest release of `spec` on 2026-10-20 and immutable (`gh release view -R altnovatrail/spec`); `gh release verify v0.2.9 -R altnovatrail/spec` confirmed it by its attestation, and the tag is on commit 5eafb1a. Д6 in table 4 of the RUN.md of `spec` also says to take the latest release at the time of the task.

— *Rejected:*
- staying at v0.1.0 until the first module: the documents check from T21 `spec` would hold any merge, T01 and T02 would read `spec` without R24 and R25 spec, and a link to a section of `spec` that is not at the tag, or a gap in the log, would pass unnoticed (Д6 in table 4 of the RUN.md of `spec`);
- v0.2.3–v0.2.8: they have `docs-check-repo`, but Д6 says to take the latest release. Rule 12 of RUN.md is not there until v0.2.7, R33 spec — until v0.2.8, and T21 `spec`, which waits for this move, is only in v0.2.9. Both `module-kit` and `tutor` already stand on v0.2.9 (the submodule `spec` on `main` of each, the GitHub API, 2026-10-20);
- moving without the devcontainer or carrying over its changes selectively: the container of `modules` is the container of `spec` (CLAUDE.md, Environment), and a difference would have to be recorded as a decision, and there is no reason for one;
- examples of citations in guillemets, as before: `docs-check-repo` checks a number in guillemets against the log, and R32 spec rejected not checking them.

— *Consequence:* T05 goes first in table 1 of RUN.md, and T01 depends on it. Card 3.10 spec does not name CI of `canvas` among those who take a release of `modules`, though the detail here names this link, — В1 in table 3 of RUN.md, «в реестр spec». The devcontainer image was not built here — Д1 in table 4 of RUN.md, until Rebuild Container. The CI files — calls of the shared workflows, `dependabot.yml`, `.sonarcloud.properties` — are written here by T21 `spec`, not by a task of this repository. — RUN.md, T05, T01–T04; CLAUDE.md; README.md; `.devcontainer/`; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 1, 2, 2.1, 2.2 and 2.3.

**R02. `modules` stands on `spec` v0.6.3 — a tag of the line 0.6, the move to which the cross-repository plan sets as the first task of the turn of `modules` (R76 spec; [COORDINATION.md](../spec/COORDINATION.md), row 3.4). The tag brings the module format 0.2 — the module's folder, its parts, the package and the index — and three modules of the first slice instead of two. The tasks of epic E1 are narrowed to what the format left to `modules`.**
- **What the tag decided for `modules`** (`git -C spec diff v0.2.9 v0.6.3`, read 2026-11-02; the parts of `modules` in Д12–Д67 of table 4 of the RUN.md of `spec`; [COORDINATION.md](../spec/COORDINATION.md), rows 3.4–3.7):
  - **three modules** (R41 spec; О-1 decided): olympiad maths 1–6, school maths for grade 3 on a workbook and short essays in English for any age. There are still two subjects — maths and writing (the amendment to R08 spec), and Spanish A1 stays a module for later. Д18 `spec`;
  - **the format** — JSON Schema 2020-12, and the author writes a module in YAML 1.2.2 with restrictions: the names of fields and of enumeration values are in snake_case, and IDs in kebab-case (R46 spec). The format text, the schemas, the examples and the test vectors lie in `spec/format/`, in English (R42, R45 spec; Т-7 closed; Д21 `spec`). The format has its own version, MAJOR.MINOR. While it is v0, the platform reads only the current version, since v0.6.0 this is 0.2, and all the official modules move to a new one in one pull request of `modules` (R44 spec; repositories 4 spec; Д20 `spec`). The modules are written in 0.2 from the first batch, and the manifest sets the digit height only if 24 px does not suit it (Д59 `spec`);
  - **the module's folder** (R46 spec; [format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 10; Д22 `spec`): `manifest.yaml`, `skill-graph.yaml`, `traps.yaml`, `techniques.yaml`, `solving-steps.yaml`, `tasks/<id>.yaml`, `lessons/<id>.yaml`, `media/`, `eval/`, a required `LICENSE` and an optional `NOTICE`. A module ID is `<publisher>/<name>` in kebab-case, `novatrail/olympiad-math`. A local ID is kebab-case, no longer than 64 characters, and the carrying over of standalone's IDs is `.` and `_` into `-`, with a check for collisions. The versions of the module and of the format are strings in quotes, and a module has one explanation language. The README and LICENSE of the repository stay at its root. Where the folders themselves lie and what they are called the format left to `modules`: this is T01;
  - **the module's parts:**
    - the skill graph: a tier is computed by the graph, the author gives the tiers their names, and a grade is the label `audience.grades` (R49 spec; О-18 decided; Д30 `spec`);
    - the anchor skills of the shared taxonomy, `core.<name>`: olympiad maths rests on the anchor skills that school maths for grade 3 teaches, not on its skills (R50 spec; Т-14 closed; Д31 `spec`);
    - the tasks and the difficulty bands `bands`: olympiad maths has the three bands of standalone, and standalone's pictures are files of `media/` with a description (R51 spec; Д32 `spec`). A picture has an opaque light background (the second 2026-11-01 amendment to R51 spec; Д58 `spec`);
    - the slot check of each answer slot is `number`, `fraction`, `choice`, `predicate` or `rubric` (R56 spec; Д37 `spec`);
    - the trap and technique catalogs, the solving steps, the tasks' solutions, the rung templates and the leak phrases (R57 spec; Д38 `spec`);
    - the lessons: the order is set by the skill graph, and for olympiad maths the source of the explanations and worked examples is the topic pages of the site of standalone (R58 spec; О-19 decided; Д39 `spec`; repositories 7 spec);
    - the pages of a workbook — by the guideline in the [format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 14. The rule of a page will come with a version 0.y that the modules' requests will bring, with T38 `spec`, and until then a page is checked only by the author's eye (the 2026-11-01 amendment to R51 spec; Д57 and Д60 `spec`);
  - **randomness, templates and solvers** (R59–R61 spec; Т-18 closed; Д42 and Д43 `spec`): the generator and the places of randomness are from `spec`, and a task template is parameters, derived numbers and a constraint on a subset of Starlark. The key of a template that has no formula is computed by a solver. The first batch does not need them: it is without templates (R76 spec). The rest of the port needs them;
  - **the package** (R62 spec; Т-15 closed; Д44 `spec`): the server part is the module's canonical view, the device part is `LICENSE`, `NOTICE` and the media files, and the package inventory names both parts by their digests. The package digest is the digest of the inventory: this is the "package manifest" of the card at v0.2.9, now with its own term (the answer to В8 `spec` in R01). The MIT notices stand only in `NOTICE`, and git does not convert the line endings of `LICENSE` and `NOTICE` (`.gitattributes`). The eval set does not go into the package: the eval runner takes the samples from the module's folder in CI of `modules`. How the packages lie in a release `modules` decides;
  - **the index** (R63 spec; Т-30 closed; Д45 `spec`): `module-index.json` in each release of `modules`, one for all the modules and cumulative. It names each published version by its package digest, with the tag of the release `vX.Y.Z` (R31 spec), the anchor skills `teaches` and the withdrawal mark `withdrawn`. `modules` decides where the withdrawals lie — as a file that is in the release's `paths` — and whether a release builds all the modules or only the changed ones. The first release builds the first index. The previous index is received and checked by a step of the shared `release.yml` of `.github`, and the recipe `release-files` of `modules` receives a file already checked (the 2026-11-01 and 2026-11-02 amendments to R63 spec; Т-35 closed; Д64 `spec`). How the file reaches the recipe is decided by T41 `.github`, and the second release needs this;
  - **the signature** (R77 spec; Т-27 closed; Д27 and Д54 `spec`; RULES.md, Hard rules): CI of `modules` takes the CLI of `module-kit` as a release binary at an exact tag and checks it with the recipe `release-verify` before its first run, rather than installing it with `go install`. The files of a release of `modules` are signed by the shared `release.yml` of `.github` no lower than v0.1.7, and before the first release `modules` has immutable releases and the ruleset of the tags `v*` by architecture 3.3 .github: without this the signature check will not accept the files;
  - **the cross-repository plan** (R76 spec; Д53 `spec`): the goal is the first application, the first build of `app` with `api`, on which the author solves tasks locally in a browser. Its module is olympiad maths: the first batch is 20–30 tasks of standalone in three bands, without templates and solvers, in lessons, with the pictures as files of `media/`, and the manifest names `adult` in the audience too. The make-up of the batch and the bindings of its tasks are decided by `modules`. Small sets of school maths and of essays are written by `modules` to check the format (T38 `spec`). There is no gate of `sim` in CI of `modules` until the first tag of `sim`. The rows of the plan: 3.4 — this move, 3.5 — epic E1, 3.6 — the content epic, which T04 will write down, 3.7 — the rest of the port;
  - **card 3.10 spec** now names CI of `canvas` among those who take a release of `modules`. So В1 of this repository is closed (Д11 and Д12 `spec`);
  - **what `modules` must know about its neighbours**, though this changes no tasks:
    - CI of `canvas` takes the tasks and the media from the packages of a release of `modules`, and until the first content — from a release of `tutor` (the 2026-10-21 and 2026-10-28 amendments to R25 spec);
    - `api` does not give a withdrawn version to new attempts, and CI of `canvas` does not build the samples page from it (R63 spec);
    - the learner model marks its state with the release of `modules` (R80 spec);
    - the reading thresholds are repeated on the volunteers' samples, and the eval sets of the slice are made of them too (R67, R68, R71 spec; the 2026-10-28 addition to П-16);
    - what lies in a sample from the samples page is П-18, and what a module may ask a learner under 18 to write about is П-20;
  - **RULES.md**, which CLAUDE.md imports: code comes with its tests, and with Codecov the merge waits for `codecov/patch` (R48 spec); an archive that the Dockerfile downloads is checked against its sha256 sum (R34 spec), and an apt package is taken from a snapshot of the Ubuntu archive (R40 spec); a file of a NovaTrail release enters code or CI only through `release-verify` (R77 spec); the normative texts for authors are in English (R42 spec); a tool's free terms are within two new bounds (the 2026-10-22 amendment to R12 spec). One round of review (R35 spec) already applies here, since commit 53edac5;
  - **the template:** the status badges at the start of the README (R48 spec; repositories 2.3 spec), rule 10 of RUN.md — with R42 spec, and the container in CLAUDE.md, Environment, — "Node.js, Go and just";
  - **devcontainer:** in the `toolchain` stage — Go 1.27.3 and `GOTOOLCHAIN=local`, each archive has a sha256 sum (R34 spec), and the comment about installing Claude Code is fixed. The Dockerfile installs no apt packages;
  - R55 spec — the vocabulary: where the context does not show whose solution it is, one writes "the task's solution" or "the learner's solution". The texts of `modules` speak of the tasks' solutions and call them so;
  - R36–R39, R43, R47, R52–R54, R64–R66, R69, R70, R72–R75, R78 and R79 spec and the amendments to R17, R22, R27 and R30–R33 spec — about other components and about `spec` itself. They change no documents of `modules`.

Both templates were built by `new-repo-draft` of their tag for `modules` of the kind `docs` in the session's folder. AGENTS.md and rules 6 and 8 of RUN.md already matched the new template (commit 53edac5). The README's badges went after the heading, and the opening of the log stayed its own;
