## The guard of the eval set

**R25. CI checks the `eval/` of each module with the recipe `just eval-check`, a step of the job "Modules", on every pull request and push to `main`, and from a fork too. The recipe holds the layout `eval/<id>.json` — the name of a file is the field `id` of its sample, and in `eval/` there are no folders, no links and no other files, except files and folders whose names start with `.`, — and, while `sample-check` has no eval-set mode, refuses any file `.json` under `eval/`, at any depth and with a name starting with `.` too. Д6 of table 4 of RUN.md is held by code, not by hands.** T44, the plan — by the executor, on the author's instruction for epic E4 (decision 4 of table 1 of RUN.md), 2026-11-08; three questions of the planning are decided by the recommendations.
- **What the recipe holds** (the 2026-11-06 amendment to R04; [contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), sections 11 and 13):
  - only the folders `modules/*/` are read: a file, a link and a name starting with `.` in `modules/` are rejected by `modules-check` (R03), and the recipe does not name them a second time, while a `modules` that is not a folder the recipe rejects itself. A module without `eval/` passes;
  - an `eval` that is a link or not a folder is a violation. In `eval/` — without descending — each entry whose name does not start with `.`: a folder, a link or a special file is a violation; a name other than `<id>.json`, where `<id>` is 1–64 lowercase Latin letters, digits and hyphens, is a violation; a file that holds not exactly one JSON value — an object with a string `id`, — or whose `id` is not its name, is a violation. The ID is matched against the name by `jq` itself, not by the shell, which would cut off a newline and a NUL at the end of `id`, and the class `[a-z]` the recipe reads in the locale `C`, where it holds no letters with diacritics;
  - **the guard:** each file under `eval/` whose name ends in `.json` — at any depth and with a name starting with `.` too — the recipe rejects, whether it is laid out right or not: `no sample enters an eval set until spec's sample-check checks eval sets (R25)`. A file whose name starts with `.` the protocol does not count as part of the eval set ([contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 13), but the repository publishes it the same way, forever, and a child sample under such a name would pass if the guard let it through. What to do with them when the mode comes, E5 will decide;
  - the refusal of one module does not stop the check of the others. The code is 1 for any violation or sample, 2 if `jq` is needed and does not start: without a file that has to be read the recipe does not call `jq`. There is no network, no key and no writing into the tree. The step of CI runs even when the step before it failed — except the build of the `toolchain` stage, — so the guard's refusal is seen from the first run.
- **Decided in the planning:**
  1. **The recipe reads the field `id` already now**, not only the names: the rule "the name is the ID" is a decided part of R04, and E5 then changes only the guard's block, while the rule of the layout is ready and checked by tests. *Rejected:* only the names — E5 would write the rule of the layout anew, in a task that changes the tag of `spec` anyway.
  2. **JSON is read by `jq`** — it is in the `toolchain` stage, and the recipes `_entries`, `_modules-check` and `release-files` already read the index and the inventory with it. It takes only `id`: the strict reading of a sample, the rules of the sample file and the rules of the eval set are `sample-check`'s, and `modules` writes no check of validity of its own (R93 spec; R04). *Rejected:* `node` — a second way to read JSON in the recipes; a parsing of the protocol of its own — the very own check that R93 spec forbids.
  3. **The guard's refusal names the file and R25, and E5 removes it:** with the move to a tag of `spec` where `sample-check` has the eval-set mode (T76 of epic E7 of `spec`), E5 replaces the marked block of the guard with the call `just -f spec/justfile sample-check --eval-set novatrail/<folder> <files>` on the samples of each module, rewrites the guard's tests and writes an amendment to this decision. *Rejected:* a flag or a variable that turns the guard off — the guard would be removed also by someone who has not moved to the mode.
- **Found in planning:** there is no step `sample-check` in CI at all, while Д6 and the row of the table of [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.2, described it as running. Until E5 the guard rejects any sample, and `sample-check` will come into CI at once in the eval-set mode.

— *Why:* a sample that got into a branch is already published, and only GitHub Support can remove it ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 4). Before the guard Д6 was held only by hands, and the file of a child's measurement — `WRITER_CHILD` with the writer code — would pass CI into the `eval/` of a module. Now none of the three modules has `eval/`, so the guard comes in without risk.

— *Rejected:* only the layout, without the guard, — Д6 would still be held by hands; nothing until the tag of `spec` with the mode — the epic would wait for someone else's release (decision 3 of table 1 of RUN.md); a check in `modules-check` — it has its own meaning, `module-kit` and the package, and `eval/` does not go into the package; the guard only on the samples of the eval set, the files `eval/*.json` whose names do not start with `.`, — so the plan decided, and the review of T44 found that a sample under a name starting with `.` would then pass CI.

— *Consequence:* Д6 stays until E5, but code holds it. — `justfile`, the recipe `eval-check`; `test/eval-check.test.mjs`; `.github/workflows/ci.yml`; CLAUDE.md; RUN.md, T44 and Д6; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.2 and 3.4.

— *Amendment 2026-11-08 (R27):* **the mode is out — in `spec` v0.9.13, — and `modules` stands on v0.9.16, but the guard stays until the first task of epic E5: the move and the replacement of the guard came apart instead of going together, as item 3 above said. The guard's refusal is now `no sample enters an eval set until CI checks it with spec's sample-check --eval-set (R25)`, not "until spec's sample-check checks eval sets": the mode exists, the step of CI does not.** The step calls the mode only for the modules that have samples in `eval/`: `sample-check` stops a call without a file with status 2. Why the move is without the step — R27.

— *Amendment 2026-11-09 (T47):* **there is no guard: in its place `just eval-check` calls `just -f spec/justfile sample-check --eval-set novatrail/<folder>` on the eval set of each module, and Д6 is closed.** So the 2026-11-08 amendment above told, as the first task of epic E5. The decisions of the planning — by the executor, on the author's instruction (decision 3 of epic E5), all by the recommendations:
- **`eval-check` calls the mode itself** — one recipe and one step of CI, the layout and the set together. *Rejected:* a separate recipe and step — two places where the files of the set are listed;
- **into the mode goes the eval set as it is named by the** [contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 13: each regular file `*.json` under `eval/`, at any depth, except those under a name starting with `.`. Links do not go: the layout rejects them, and a link outward would stop `sample-check` with status 2. A module without such files does not call the mode: `sample-check` stops a call without a file with status 2;
- **the mode is called even on a violation of the layout**, so that one run shows everything. The code of the recipe is the greatest of what failed: 1 — a violation of the layout or a refusal of the mode, 2 — `jq`, the tools of `spec` or the mode could not do their part, and without `jq` the recipe goes on through the other modules. The tools of `spec` are installed by a separate call `just -f spec/justfile _tools` before the first set: `npm ci` fails with status 1, like a refusal of the mode, and otherwise they could not be told apart. After it a status above 2 is also 2. A folder whose name gives no module ID stops the mode with status 2, and `modules-check` rejects it in the same job;
- **the tests run the real `sample-check`** — the `spec/` of a made-up repository is a link to the submodule of this one, — and a substitute `spec/justfile` is needed only for the form of the call and the statuses. A call costs 0.4 s (measured 2026-11-09). *Rejected:* a substitute everywhere — the test would not see what the mode accepts and rejects in fact;
- **there is nothing in `eval/` whose name starts with `.`** — the 2026-11-09 amendment to R04.

The recipe needs the network only for `sample-check`: it installs the tools of `spec/tools` from the lock file through npm, as `docs-check-repo` does. There is no key, so a pull request from a fork passes it.

— *Amendment 2026-11-10 (T58):* **`eval-check` calls `sample-check --eval-set` on a module's eval set 50 files at a time, in order, and the recipe's code is the greatest of all the calls.** One call on 631 files of synthetic samples of `olympiad-math` crashes Node with code 134, and chunks of 50 pass (`canvas`'s answer to В36, 2026-11-10). Among the rules of the set that the mode checks, only one concerns several files at once: two samples never have one ID (`evalSetBreaches` in `spec/tools/contracts/sample.mjs` at this repository's tag of `spec`; [contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 13). The layout already holds it: `eval/` is flat, and the ID is the file's name in it, — so the chunks lose not one rule. The crash itself is in the tool of `spec`, and `modules` reports it by row В37 of table 3 of RUN.md: with the fix the recipe will return to one call. The executor's decision in the planning of T58, on the author's instruction (decision 2 of epic E8).
- *Rejected:* one call — the synthetic samples of T60 would stop it; `xargs -n 50` — it replaces the codes 1 and 2 with its own 123, and a refusal could not be told from a failure.
— `justfile`, the recipe `eval-check`; `test/eval-check.test.mjs`, `test/helpers.mjs`.

— *Amendment 2026-11-10 (T59):* **the step "eval-match" of the job "Modules", the recipe `just eval-match`, checks each sample of the eval set against the package that `module-kit build` built at this commit. A synthetic sample it draws anew with the generator of `canvas` from the stage (R29) — with the tag from the file, not with the newest, — and compares it with the file in everything but `task.modules.version` and `task.modules.package_digest`. For a person's sample it checks `task.open_view_digest` against the digest of the task's open view — for a template, of the instance by `task.seed` — by `module-kit open` over the commit's package. A sample that did not match is refusal 1 with the file and the task: a synthetic sample is to be redrawn (`just synthetic`), a person's sample removed.** So what the 2026-11-06 amendment to R04 required holds: a pull request that changes a task's open view remakes its synthetic samples in the same pull request and removes the people's samples ([contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 13). The executor's decisions in the planning of T59, on the author's instruction (decision 2 of epic E8):
- **not to compare the version and the digest of the package** — [contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 13: "The tag, the version and the package's digest that the sample names say only what it was made on, and are not compared, but the digest of the task's open view MUST be the sample's". Otherwise an edit of any task of the module — a new package digest — would refuse all its synthetic samples. The tag is compared, but matches by itself: the drawing is made with the file's tag. *Rejected:* a byte-for-byte comparison — against the same section of the contracts text, and hundreds of samples would be redrawn without an edit of their tasks; a check of synthetic samples only by the digest — the drawing is changed both by the task's picture and by a new version of the generator, while the open view may stay the same;
- **the digest for a person's sample — from `module-kit open`**, option (c) of T59, and not (a), recommended there, the command `--digest` of the generator of `canvas` (R68 `canvas`). A new argument: `--digest` gives a template no digest ("no open view, being a template"), and the author's samples, T62, are above all on templates. `open` opens an instance by the seed and gives the digest of each task by the same projection by which the runner will read the task, and T86 `canvas` checked its projection against it (architecture 3.6 `module-kit`; R93 spec). The index for `open` is `module-kit index --first` over the commit's package, and the command requires the seed `--seed`, so without seeds `--seed 0` goes: it does not touch the instances of templates. Checked 2026-11-10: the digest of `times-tables-d3` is the same at `open` and at the generator of `canvas`. *Rejected:* (a) — the templates are not checked; (b) a projection of its own — a third implementation of the open view;
- **the generator is called in groups** — by tag and no more than 50 calls, like the chunks of `eval-check`: it holds all the samples of a call in memory. A chunk that the generator rejected the recipe draws anew one sample at a time, and each file gets its own reason from the generator, word for word, with code 1 — for example, the task became a template or disappeared;
- **what the recipe reads** — only fields, with `jq`: the origin (`synthetic` or `person`), the module, the task, the seed, the tag and the digest; it does not print a person's sample. A file outside the layout `eval/<id>.json`, a file without these fields, a sample of another module and a synthetic sample without a seed or a tag are refusal 1, though `eval-check` rejects them too: so not one sample passes without the check. Seeds up to 2^64 the recipe compares as strings, not as numbers. A module without samples is not built. The code is the greatest over the modules, and it is never above 2: 1 — a mismatch, 2 — `module-kit`, the generator or `jq` could not. There is neither network nor key: a pull request from a fork passes the step;
- **the measurement of 2026-11-10** in a temporary copy: 631 synthetic samples of `olympiad-math` (322 MB) — `eval-match` in 42 s, `eval-check` in 2 min 48 s; the job "Modules" has a limit of 20 minutes.
— `justfile`: `eval-match`, `_match-synthetic`, `_match-people`, and the checks of the CLI and of the generator are moved out of `_synthetic` into `_pinned-module-kit` and `_generator`; `.github/workflows/ci.yml`; `test/eval-match.test.mjs`, `test/helpers.mjs` (`pinGenerator`), `test/synthetic.test.mjs`.

— *Amendment 2026-11-14 (R32):* **`eval-check` calls `sample-check --eval-set` once per module, on all the files of its eval set, in order: since T116 `spec`, in `spec` v0.17.2, the check holds whole only the file it reads, and one call checks hundreds of large samples with no batches ([contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 11). This replaces the chunks of 50 of the T58 amendment; the rule that no two samples share an ID the tool now holds itself, and the flat layout holds it as before.** T116 is the answer to В37 (Д197 in the `DEBT.md` of `spec`); `spec` tested one call on 631 copies of an example essay of 257 KB, not on a real set, and T68 measured the case of 2026-11-10 again: on a model of 631 synthetic samples of `olympiad-math`, 323 MB, `sample-check` of v0.16.3 stops Node with 134 in one call, and `eval-check` with v0.17.2 passes in 97 s (measurements 3). Д7 is closed. The groups of 50 in which `eval-match` calls the generator of `canvas` stay: the generator holds every sample of a call in memory (the T59 amendment). — `justfile`, the recipe `eval-check`; `test/eval-check.test.mjs`; [measurements.md](measurements.md), section 3; `.claude/rules/eval.md`; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.2.

## The requests to E5

**R26. `modules` chose what the eval-set organizer and the module's author view a sample with before the push — the samples page of `canvas`, by the mode "open a file": the same build without the network shows the strokes of the final geometry, the crops and the texts of the markup, changing nothing, saving nothing and calling no AI, — and asks for it by row В33. The synthetic samples for the module's tasks it asks of the generator of `canvas` by row В32: on the module package that `module-kit build` built at the commit where the samples land, with the source `modules`. Both rows are «в реестр spec», table 3 of RUN.md. While there is no viewing, people's samples are not added to `eval/`. The author's declaration on the page need not be asked for: `canvas` has already taken it.** T45, the plan — by the executor, on the author's instruction for epic E4 (decision 4 of the epic: table 1 of RUN.md, and after the epic's outcome its archive `docs/plan/E4.md`), 2026-11-08; both choices — by the recommendations, and the package of the synthetic samples — by a finding of the review. [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.4, left the choice of viewing to epic E4.
- **What was read** (2026-11-08: `canvas` — `main` 316349d, `spec` — v0.9.10 and `main` 219c3cf):
  - neither `canvas` nor `spec` has a viewing of a sample file, and `spec` does not plan one. The protocol requires the organizer to look for a name in the markup too — the texts of the parts and the essay's truth are published with the sample, "so the organizer looks for a name in them as on the sheet" ([contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 11);
  - the generator of synthetic samples of `canvas` — `src/eval/synthetic.ts`, the recipe `just synthetic` — draws 16 samples from the seeds 101–116 for the tasks of the file of open views of `tutor` v0.1.10, sheets and workbook pages, without essays (R49 and R50 canvas). An eval-set sample of a module is a task of its own module with the source `modules` and the module ID, and the tag, the version and the package digest that it names "say only what it was made on, and are not compared": only the digest of the task's open view is checked ([contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 13);
  - the author's declaration for the purpose «eval-набор» ("eval set") and the words «передать файл организатору eval-набора» ("pass the file to the eval-set organizer") `canvas` took as an input of T20 of its epic E5, by the move to `spec` v0.9.8 (R47 canvas; Д80 in table 4 of the RUN.md of `spec`). Took, but has not done yet: E5 waits for T20 `canvas`, and E6 for its words about passing the file;
  - where the runner's report may lie, the [contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 14, "Where a report goes", says: the run and the published report follow the provider's terms, and the open log of CI publishes what it prints (R95 spec; R65 spec). Д97 `spec`, which [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.4, referred to, is closed.
- **Viewing — the samples page of `canvas`** (В33). Why: `canvas` already reads this file with its `src/eval/` and draws these strokes and crops (R48 canvas), the page works without the network and does not call AI, and there is one implementation for everyone who adds samples. This is a request, not a ready tool: until `spec` has answered and `canvas` has made it, there is nothing to view a person's sample with, and such samples are not added to `eval/` — the authors' samples need viewing in E5, the volunteers' samples in E6. *Rejected:* a recipe of `spec` that writes a page from the file — `spec` holds contracts and checks, not an interface, and would draw the strokes a second time; a recipe of `modules`' own — a second reader and drawer of the protocol in a repository that has no code of the platform.
- **Synthetic samples — the generator of `canvas` on the commit's package** (В32; decision 2 of epic E4):
  - the tasks and their open views — from the module package, as `module-kit build` writes it, at the commit where the samples land. The package is given to the generator by the call: this is a new link of `canvas` with the module package already at stage 1, and the answer of `spec` edits [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 1 and 3.3 and the cards of `modules` and `canvas` in architecture 3 spec;
  - the sample writes the source `modules` — the module ID, the version and the digest of this package and the tag of a release of `modules`, — `PURPOSE_EVAL_SET`, Apache-2.0, the ID from the seed and the markup. The tag at the source is required, and the commit's package has none: which tag it names `spec` decides, and `modules` proposes the tag of the newest release — the tag is not checked ([contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 13);
  - the tasks and the seeds are named by the call. The ID is derived from the seed, and two samples of a set do not share an ID, so the call takes seeds that are not yet in the module's eval set, and `modules` puts the files as `eval/<id>.json` (R25);
  - the kinds of tasks — those that the generator draws, sheets and the workbook; essays — when `canvas` starts drawing them.

  Why the commit and not a release: the runner of stage 2 runs a module whose package has changed ([architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.4), and a pull request that changes a task's open view remakes or removes its samples in the same pull request (the 2026-11-06 amendment to R04). With synthetic samples on the commit's package it remakes them, and the task is checked on synthetic samples at the same time as it changes. *Rejected:* the release's package — a pull request would remove the synthetic samples of exactly the task that it changes, until the next release; the generator as a library that a script of `modules` calls — a second wrapper of the code that draws from a seed, which stays with `canvas` (R38 spec); eval sets without synthetic samples — only people's samples.
- **The report in the open log of CI** is a question for the [contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 14, and R95 spec, not for Д97 `spec`. What CI prints from the report, and for which providers, epic E6 decides together with the runner: the providers' terms are not retold here, they will be read from the primary source then.

— *Why now:* `spec` reads the rows «в реестр spec» on `main` (R81 spec), and the sooner they are there, the sooner `canvas` takes up the synthetic samples and the viewing that E5 waits for.

— *Consequence:* E5 also waits for В32, В33 and T20 `canvas`; E6 for В33, the words of T20 `canvas` about passing the file and the decision on what CI prints from the report. — RUN.md, tables 2 and 3, T45; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.4.

Перейди на новую версию spec».
- **What the tag decided for `modules`** (`git -C spec diff v0.9.10 v0.9.16`, read 2026-11-08; Д109, Д115, Д121 and Д128 in table 4 of the RUN.md of `spec` and table 5 there):

The card architecture 3.10 spec names it on every pull request, and from a fork too. Д6 is closed by the CI step that will take the guard's place;

  - **the rows of table 3 of RUN.md** — where the answer to each lay:
    - В28–В31 — R108 spec and the 2026-11-08 amendment to R82 spec, format 0.4 in epic E6 of `spec` (Д121): a flag on a choice by hand only for options that are words; the check kind `time` — decided otherwise than `modules` recommended, which asked for time as a choice by the flag; placeholders in the path of a template's picture; the closed field `leading_question`. The format has no rules yet: `modules` will take them by the move to a tag of the line 0.10;

The format version is the same. `just test` and `just modules-check` at v0.9.16 are green, but `modules-check` calls `module-kit` v0.2.3, pinned in the Dockerfile, and it does not hold these clarifications yet. Whether they touch the modules — for example, a solver that changes a global list — the move to a `module-kit` that implements them will tell;

- **What changed here:**
  - `spec/` — at v0.9.16, commit c495b88. The tag in CLAUDE.md, README.md, the opening of RUN.md and the status of [architecture.md](architecture.md);
  - the template: `repo-template` of the kind `go` got gopls in the `devcontainer` stage and the settings of VS Code (T80 `spec`), and `tools/new-repo` their build. `modules` is of the kind `docs`: the templates `new-repo-draft modules docs 3.10` of the tags v0.9.10 and v0.9.16 differ only by the tag, so the move requires neither a merge nor a rebuild of the container. `modules` has enough Go of its own — `tools/port`, — but its devcontainer has no Go extension, and nobody installs gopls; the kind `go` with gopls by the lock file is a decision of its own, if it is asked for; `.devcontainer`, `tools/toolchain` and RULES.md did not change (`git -C spec diff --stat`);

## The tag `spec` v0.12.0 and format 0.4

**R28. `modules` stands on `spec` v0.12.0, the latest release of `spec`, and CI on `module-kit` v0.3.0, the first release with format 0.4. All three modules move to format 0.4 with versions 0.2.0, and only by the module manifest: they need no other edit to pass 0.4. The releases of `modules` move to the line 0.2, and the merge puts out v0.2.0 — the first release with packages of 0.4. What 0.4 gives the content — the word flag, the kind `time`, the pictures of instances, leading questions, lists of steps and examples on the workbook — stays for epic E7.** The author's instruction 2026-11-09: «релиз module-kit произошёл. переходи на spec v0.12.0», — as the move R27, without an epic and a task. The release line is the author's answer the same day, by the recommendation.
- **Why together.** `module-kit` v0.3.0 reads format 0.4 «и только его: модуль 0.3 отклоняется по `manifest.format-version`» (R52 module-kit, read 2026-11-09, `gh api` at the tag v0.3.0). Since `spec` v0.10.0 the format's examples and vectors in `spec/format/` are 0.4, and the recipes' tests stand on them: at v0.12.0 with `module-kit` v0.2.3, 26 tests of 95 fail. So the tag of `spec`, the CI binary and the modules' format move in one pull request, as Д135 in table 4 of the RUN.md of `spec` requires: `modules` — after the release of `module-kit` with 0.4. So В34 of table 3 of RUN.md, which `spec` did not manage to record, is closed too: the divergence of Д135 and Д149 left with the move.
- **What the tag decided for `modules`** (`git -C spec diff v0.9.16 v0.12.0`, read 2026-11-09):

The modules do not take them yet: this is epic E7;

  - privacy and consent (T92–T94 `spec`), the format of the deployment configuration and the edit of admission — about other components and about the volunteer's form, which E6 waits for. They do not change the documents of `modules`.
- **`module-kit` v0.3.0** — [altnovatrail/module-kit, release v0.3.0](https://github.com/altnovatrail/module-kit/releases/tag/v0.3.0), immutable, read 2026-11-09. `just module-kit-verify`: "SHA256SUMS: module-kit v0.3.0, signed by the shared release.yml of .github v0.1.15", the linux sums — in the `ARG` of `.devcontainer/Dockerfile` (R10). The forms of the call of `check`, `build`, `index` and `diff` in the `docs/architecture.md` of `module-kit` are the same as at v0.2.3 (`gh api` at both tags, 2026-11-09), and the recipes need no edit.

The modules stay in 0.y, like the format v0 ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 27).
- **The release line 0.2** — `RELEASE_LINE` in `.github/workflows/release.yml`. The newest index now carries packages of 0.4, which a reader of format 0.3 will not read, and in 0.y an incompatible change is carried by the minor number, as with the line 0.3 of `module-kit` (R52 module-kit). The entries of the versions 0.1.x stay in the index where they were published. A trial build `just release-files v0.2.0` over the index of `v0.1.4`, accepted by `release-verify`, gave three archives 0.2.0, and the former entries stayed in their places.
- **The automatic releases v0.1.2–v0.1.4.** A merge that changes the release's paths — `justfile` among them — puts out a release after CI on `main` (R17). So v0.1.2 (T44), v0.1.3 (R27) and v0.1.4 (T47) came out, without new versions of the modules: each entry of the index stayed in its own release. This is the behaviour of R17, but T44, R27 and T47 did not name it — and this move names it.

The template: for the kind `docs`, `repo-template`, `tools/new-repo`, `.devcontainer`, `tools/toolchain` and RULES.md did not change from v0.9.16 to v0.12.0 (`git -C spec diff --stat`); only the comment of the gopls step of the kind `go` changed;

## The generator of synthetic samples

**R29. The synthetic samples of a module's eval set are written by the recipe `just synthetic <module> <task>:<seed>...` with the generator of `canvas` — the file `synthetic-module_v0.3.6.mjs` of the release `canvas` v0.3.6. The file stands in the `toolchain` stage of `.devcontainer/Dockerfile` by tag and sha256 sum, like the CLI of `module-kit` (R10), and is run by the Node of this stage. CI checks the release's signature with the recipe `canvas-verify` on every run. The recipe builds the module package with `module-kit build` into a temporary folder and calls the generator with the tag of the newest release of `modules`; the generator writes the samples into the module's `eval/`. This is the second difference of the container of `modules` from the container of `spec`.** T58, the plan — by the executor, on the author's instruction for epic E8 (decision 2 of epic E8), 2026-11-10; the choice is option (a) of decision 3 of epic E8, by the recommendation.
- **The generator** — `canvas`'s answer to the request В36: R68 `canvas`, every release of `canvas` since v0.3.6 carries `synthetic-module_v<X.Y.Z>.mjs` — one ES module for Node 24 without npm packages beside it, with the signature of the shared `release.yml` and in `SHA256SUMS` ([altnovatrail/canvas, release v0.3.6](https://github.com/altnovatrail/canvas/releases/tag/v0.3.6), read 2026-11-10). It has two forms of the call:
  - `node <file> --module <package> <tag> <folder> <task>:<seed>...` writes synthetic samples into the folder. All the calls it checks before writing: if one has not passed, it writes nothing. A person's sample it never overwrites. The exit codes: 0 — written, 1 — a refusal with a reason for each call, 2 — a call not of the form;
  - `node <file> --digest <package> <task>...` prints the task's ID and the digest of its open view — by the same projection by which a sample names it. This is the second request of В36, for T59.

  The file asks for no network: it has only `node:fs`, `node:path` and `node:crypto`.
- **How it gets into `modules`.** In the `toolchain` stage stand `ARG CANVAS_VERSION=v0.3.6` and `ARG CANVAS_SYNTHETIC_SHA256` — one sum for both architectures, because there is one file. The same `fetch` that downloads the rest checks the sum before the install (R34 spec) and puts the file into `/usr/local/lib/canvas/synthetic-module_v0.3.6.mjs`. The sum is taken from the `SHA256SUMS` of the release, which `just -f spec/justfile release-verify canvas v0.3.6` accepted before the sum was written down (R77 spec): `bc7ff50b1d007b9f0b45b059efae995a10efeed6b07a5222656358becfd540a8`. The Node of the stage is 24.21.0, as the generator requires. Dependabot does not see the file: the tag and the sum are raised by hand, together, and the container is rebuilt after that.
- **The recipes** (`justfile`):
  - `synthetic` passes the call to `_synthetic` with an empty tag, and that one takes the tag of the newest release of `modules` from `_newest-release` — after all the checks that need no network: a typo in the module's name or a container that is not rebuilt is named by its own reason and does not go to the network. If there is no release, this is a refusal with status 2;
  - `_newest-release` — the loop moved out of `_published`. The tags `vX.Y.Z` are taken from `git ls-remote`, the newest is the first that has a `module-index.json`; a tag whose release did not come out is skipped. `_published` now calls it and behaves as before. The index's signature is not needed for synthetic samples: the recipe takes only the tag from it, and the index file enters neither code nor CI;
  - `_synthetic <module> <tag> <calls>...` checks in order: `modules/<module>` is a folder, and the name is not empty; `eval/` there is a folder or does not exist, but is not a link and not a file, otherwise the generator would write the samples past the module; `module-kit --version` is the one pinned in the Dockerfile; the sum of the generator's file is `CANVAS_SYNTHETIC_SHA256`. Otherwise a refusal with status 2 and the words "rebuild the container": a container not rebuilt after the tag was raised would write not what CI would make. Then `module-kit build` into a temporary folder, the package ID is `novatrail/<module>` (R03), and the generator `--module <package> <tag> modules/<module>/eval <calls>`. Its lines, the reasons of its refusals and its exit code are the recipe's: a task that the generator rejects is named by its reason. The generator's folder is set by `CANVAS_LIB` — for the stub in the tests;
  - `canvas-verify` downloads the release's `SHA256SUMS` and its bundle by the public address, without a token, checks the signature through `release-verify canvas <tag>`, and `_canvas-sum` checks the generator's line against `CANVAS_SYNTHETIC_SHA256`.
- **CI** (`ci.yml`) — the job "canvas signature", `just canvas-verify`, on every pull request and push to `main`; the job "Modules" waits for it together with "module-kit signature". The recipe of synthetic samples itself CI does not call yet — this is the check of T59.
- **The tests.**
  - `test/synthetic.test.mjs` holds the form of the call: build into an empty folder, the generator after it, the generator's arguments in order. It also checks that the generator's status and reasons pass through unchanged and that the recipe refuses in each of the cases: an old sum, no file, another `module-kit`, a refusal of `module-kit`, another ID, a call not of the form. All of this is on a stub of the generator whose sum the Dockerfile of a made-up repository writes.
  - One test runs the real generator of the stage and the real `module-kit` on a copy of `grade-3-math`: the sample passes `eval-check`, and a second call with the same seed gives the same file byte for byte.
  - `test/published.test.mjs` holds `_newest-release` on stubs of `git` and `curl`: a tag whose index answers 404 is skipped, another failure is status 2, not "there is no release".
  - `test/canvas-verify.test.mjs` holds `_canvas-sum` on a made-up `SHA256SUMS` and Dockerfile, without the network, as `test/module-kit-verify.test.mjs` holds the sums of R10.
- **What the generator does with the packages 0.3.0** from `v0.2.1` (a run of 2026-11-10, outside the repository):
  - `grade-3-math` — draws 12 of 12 tasks: all are workbook pages;
  - `olympiad-math` — 631 of 775: 122 tasks with a choice it rejects with the words "a choice", and 22 templates with "a template: its instance by a seed is module-kit's";
  - the kind `time` and `notation: word` gave no refusals;
  - `--digest` gives a digest to 753 tasks of `olympiad-math` of 775 — all but the 22 templates, — and to 12 of 12 of `grade-3-math`;
  - a repeated call with the same seed gives the same bytes.

  A sample of a workbook page of `grade-3-math` weighs from 6 KB to 1.3 MB, the median is 625 KB (12 samples); the sheet of `olympiad-math` `cal-12-d1-4` is 540 KB. The volume of synthetic samples is decided by T60.

— *Why:* a synthetic sample of a module's eval set is a sample of a task of its own module on the package of the commit where it lands (R110 spec). `canvas` has the generator (T86 `canvas`), and now as a signed file of a release, so `modules` takes it by the same path as `module-kit`: an exact version, a sum in the Dockerfile and a signature in CI (R77 spec; R10).

— *Rejected:*
- a clone of `canvas` at the tag and commit, `npm ci` and a build by `tsc` in a temporary folder — option (b) of decision 3 of epic E8. There is a file of the release, and a clone pulls npm dependencies that nothing holds by a signature;
- downloading the generator in the recipe on every call — the network on every call and a second path beside R10, while CI builds the stage anyway;
- the npm package `@altnovatrail/canvas` — the generator is not in it (decision 3 of epic E8);
- the tag from `gh release list` — a token is needed, and `_published` already finds the newest release without one.

— *Consequence:* after the merge one has to rebuild the container (Rebuild Container), otherwise `just synthetic` refuses with the words "rebuild the container"; CI builds the stage itself. Row В36 is removed. The check of T59 and the synthetic samples of T60 stand on this recipe. — `.devcontainer/Dockerfile`; `justfile`; `.github/workflows/ci.yml`; `test/synthetic.test.mjs`, `test/canvas-verify.test.mjs`; CLAUDE.md; RUN.md, T58 and table 3; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.2 and 3.4; the T58 amendment to R25.

— *Amendment 2026-11-10 (T59):* **CI calls the generator too: the step "eval-match" draws the synthetic samples of the eval set anew with it and compares them with the files (the T59 amendment to R25).** The item "CI" above — "the recipe of synthetic samples itself CI does not call yet" — stays as it is: CI calls not `synthetic` but the generator. The checks of the generator's sum and of the version of `module-kit` are moved out of `_synthetic` into `_pinned-module-kit` and `_generator`, and both recipes call them, so the synthetic samples are written and checked by one and the same file of the generator.

## The tag `spec` v0.16.3 and the token savings

**R31. `modules` stands on `spec` v0.16.3, the latest release of `spec`, through v0.15.1–v0.16.2. The translation of `docs/` into English is T65–T67. The move does not change the versions of the modules and does not touch the release's paths: the merge puts out no release.** The author's instruction 2026-11-12: «переходи на spec последней версии». The author's answer the same day: the whole task of row 7.9 — now, without waiting for the pilot of `module-kit` (row 7.1), split by rule 11, and the executor brings it to the merge into `main` themselves.
- **What the tag decided for `modules`** (`git -C spec diff v0.15.0 v0.16.3`, the rows of the `DEBT.md` of `spec` that name `modules`, read 2026-11-12):
  - **R143 spec and R145 spec** — the token savings of epic E10 of `spec`. One task is one session, and the hook `UserPromptSubmit` from `spec/tools/session/` stops a phrase that starts another. CLAUDE.md is thin, and the checks of the parts are in the rules of `.claude/rules/` by `paths`. The body of a done task goes to the archive at once, table 4 to `DEBT.md`. An external fact is a record of `docs/sources.md`, the outcome of a measurement goes to `docs/measurements.md`. `docs/` are in English, and a document still in Russian stays so until its own task of translation. `docs-check` measures the size budgets when the repository has `docs-budgets.json` (RULES.md spec; the 2026-11-11 and 2026-11-12 amendments to R19 spec). What each repository takes is T123 `spec`; for `modules` the table of differences has: «хука нет; три раздела architecture; запись R03 — 41 КБ»;
  - **Д215** — a part rule of `spec/.claude/rules/` Claude Code would load into a session of `modules` as soon as the session reads a file of that rule's part in `spec/`. The line `"**/modules/spec/.claude/rules/**"` in `claudeMdExcludes` is needed;
  - **the answers to В38 and В39** — the edit of the rows Д173 and Д170 of `spec` on 2026-11-11, by the rows' recommendations. Both rows are removed, and Д8 is rewritten by what remains (the R31 amendment to R30);
  - **the tag of `sim` with `time`** — v0.1.9 (2026-11-11) and v0.1.10 (2026-11-12): rows 4.10 and 4.12 of COORDINATION.md of `spec`, `gh release list -R altnovatrail/sim`. The gate of Д5 waits only for its own task, and so does the measurement of Д8;

## The tag `spec` v0.17.2

**R32. `modules` stands on `spec` v0.17.2, the latest release of `spec`, through v0.16.4–v0.17.1. The move is T68, the first task of epic E9, which stands on the 2026-11-14 amendment to R76 spec. The move does not change the versions of the modules. The merge changes the `justfile`, a path of the release, and puts out a release without new archives.** The author's instruction 2026-11-14: the first task of epic E9 is the move to the latest release of `spec`. The author's answers the same day in its planning: rule 9 by the template, and the executor brings the task to the merge into `main`.
- **What the tag decided for `modules`** (`git -C spec diff v0.16.3 v0.17.2`, the rows of the `DEBT.md` of `spec` that name `modules` or the eval-set organizer, read 2026-11-14):

## The module of the check board

**R33. The module of the check board of `tutor` is `novatrail/check-board` 0.1.0 in `modules/check-board/`, on a sheet: the eleven tasks of the board in its order, from the banks of `tutor` at `v0.1.35`, each as `internal/task.Sheet` builds it (R68 tutor); three skills by the kinds of the banks and a fourth that is not assessed, 22 traps, one lesson of all eleven tasks and three synthetic samples. The explanation language is Russian, the audience `adult` alone, one band without grades; no skill teaches an anchor skill. There is no `NOTICE`.** The author's answers 2026-11-14 in the planning of T69 (RUN.md, T69, «Решено автором»), all eight by the recommendations, and the rest by the plan that the author approved. The make-up is in [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.10.
- **The source.** The banks `eval/bank.json` and `eval/free-bank.json` of `tutor` at `v0.1.35`, its latest tag on 2026-11-14; the board, the banks and `internal/task` are as at `v0.1.34`. A script writes the tasks from the banks, not a hand, and the open view of each of the eleven, which `module-kit open` gives, is the JSON of the task in `eval/open-tasks.json` of `tutor` at that tag.
- **The tasks.** A paragraph of the statement; a handwriting sheet of the default grid; one answer slot `anywhere` with the check `number` and the value of the bank's key, as `sample.KeyOf` of `tutor` takes it (`x = 2` is `2`); the solution a paragraph per line; the bank's traps in `traps`, without trap values; the bank's leak phrases but those in English, and after them the key in words — in `posts`, whose statement frees the key, also «5 столбов». The closed fields that `Sheet` fills with stand-ins are the module's: the skill by the kind of the bank, the band `board` and a difficulty by the number of actions of the bank's solution — `houses` 1, `chain-1` and `pages` 3, `album` 4, the other seven 2.
- **Skills** — `linear-equation`, `calculation` and `free-solution`, of the assessability `verdict`, the kinds of the banks as `Sheet` names them; and `writing-on-a-sheet`, «Решение на листе», `none`, a supporting skill of every task and a skill of the lesson. No prerequisites, no `teaches`, no `dependencies`.
- **Traps** — 22, those that the tasks name, with the bank's ID, and the bank's description in impersonal words, which name no gender; the advice is the module's own: for the learner, addressed with «ты», with no verb in the past tense singular and no digit.
- **The lesson** — one, `board`, «Задачи доски по порядку»: the eleven tasks in the order of `internal/board/tasks.go` of `tutor`, without an explanation.
- **The eval set** — three synthetic samples by the rule of T60, a pair of a primary skill and the band, the task first by ID: `chain-1:1`, `album:2` and `equation-1:3`. They name `v0.2.6`, the newest release when they were drawn (R110 spec), whose index does not hold the module: of a module's eval set the runner takes the task from the commit's package, and the tag is not compared ([the text of the contracts](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 13).
- **The name** `check-board` names the board whose tasks the module holds, not the subject and the audience, as R03 asks of the names of the slice: the module is of no slice, and its ID tells what it is for (the T69 amendment to R03).
- **The manifest**: the title «Задачи на листе», `mathematics`, the band «Задачи доски», the authors «Авторы NovaTrail», and the rung templates of the other three modules in Russian, without digits.
- **Version** — `0.1.0`, by the argument of R13.

— *Why:*
- **Tasks as `Sheet` builds them**: the board is the same thing on every try (R19 tutor), and the eval set is to check a tag of `tutor` on the tasks that it measured. The same open view lets `tutor` take its tasks from the package later, by a task of its own.
- **Russian**: the statements, the solutions and the leak phrases of the banks are Russian, and the measurements of `tutor` are on them; a translation would be other tasks. `tutor` admits the tutor's words in Russian, 30 words a hint (R70 spec; `words/words.go` of `tutor`), and its reading gives the number of the final answer without the unknown and without the words after it (`internal/reading/reading.go` of `tutor`), so the keys need no `units`.
- **The English leak phrases left out**: every text of a module is in its explanation language ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 10), and the tutor's words in this module are Russian. **The key in words added**: a Russian text writes numbers so, and the leak check reads no number in words ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 20); the tutor's words are checked by it too (R54 spec).
- **`adult` alone**: the board is the author's, and its tasks and traps come from the measurement of an adult. Adding an age category later is a minor version ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 27).
- **No anchor skill**: the module stands beside the first slice, not in it, and nothing builds on `core.linear-equations`, which v1 keeps only if a module of the slice names it (R50 spec).
- **The skill that is not assessed**: the engine adds no segment to a lesson whose skills are not all `verdict`, and skips none ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 21). The engine of the first slice changes no lesson anyway (architecture 3.8 spec), but epic E7 of `engine` would give a task of consolidation at the end of a segment of a lesson whose skills are all `verdict` (row 4.14 of [COORDINATION.md](../spec/COORDINATION.md)), and the board is to be the same path every try.
- **No explanation**: the board has none, and one would be a step more on every run and more text to keep from leaks.
- **The difficulty by actions**: a rule that anyone can check on the bank's solution, and it spreads the tasks over the scale.
- **Three synthetic samples**: the rule of T60, by which the other eval sets were chosen. On a sheet the generator writes sums of its own, not the task's solution, so a sample per task would add size and nothing of the task.
- **The advice of the traps the module's own**: the banks give none, and the trap catalog requires it ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 16). **The description in impersonal words**: the learner's progress shows it ([format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 16), and the bank's names a gender — «перенёс», «ошибся».

— *Rejected:*
- the IDs `board-math` and `sheet-math-adults`;
- a translation into English; the audience of every age;
- `teaches: [core.linear-equations]`, with a row «в реестр spec»;
- a lesson of the three skills with a verdict alone; an explanation before the tasks;
- the difficulty 3 for all, and the difficulty 1 of `Sheet` for all;
- a synthetic sample for each task;
- trap values from the bank's `false_keys`: the bank does not say which trap a false key shows, and `Sheet` takes none;
- `units` for the answers with words: the reading of `tutor` gives the number alone, and `Sheet` has none;
- the English leak phrases kept; the leak phrases of the bank alone, without the key in words;
- the bank's descriptions word for word, in the past tense of one gender.

— *Consequence:*
- `module-kit check` passes without violations or warnings, and so does `just modules-check`, `check-board` 0.1.0 as a first version; `just eval-check` and `just eval-match` hold its three samples. On a copy of the module, «на один больше» in the advice of `gaps-for-posts` breaks `task.no-leaks` for `posts`, and a number in the lesson's title breaks `lesson.no-leaks`;
- [architecture.md](architecture.md): sections 1, 3, 3.1, 3.4, 3.7 and the new 3.10; README.md — the list of the modules and the status; CLAUDE.md — the role;
- RUN.md, table 3 — the row В40 «в реестр spec»: the card architecture 3.10 spec names the three modules of the slice alone;
- the merge puts out the release `v0.2.7` of `modules` with the one new archive, `check-board` 0.1.0 (R17), which rows 5.10 and 6.17 of [COORDINATION.md](../spec/COORDINATION.md) wait for. The author's samples are T70.

— RUN.md, T69; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.10; `modules/check-board/`; the T69 amendment to R03.
